# Implementation Plan: notification-tray-and-hidden-session-gate

**Feature**: One delivery gate that keeps routine hidden-session notifications off every channel, a capped/bulk-manageable toast stack, a non-modal notification tray, and a read-only view for hidden sessions that do notify.
**Date**: 2026-10-07 (third plan revision, Phase 3 iteration 3: iteration 1 = gate/prune/read-only repairs, iteration 2 = UX reconciliation, iteration 3 = operator decisions O1-O6 plus the second-round architecture and adversarial re-reviews); patched 2026-10-08 in SDD Phase 4 (pre-mortem P1/P2/P3, consistency and validation findings; see "Repair log (Phase 4)"); **patched 2026-10-09 in triad iteration 1** (UX B1-B3, Eng B1-B2, product and UX gaps, operator decisions O7 confirmed and O8 appetite; see "Repair log (Triad iteration 1)"); **patched 2026-10-09 in review-repair iteration 1** (the focused re-review's 7 blockers and its concern groups; see "Repair log (Review-repair iteration 1)"); **patched 2026-10-09 in review-repair iteration 2** (focused re-review iteration 2 found 2 blockers on the architecture side and 0 on the adversarial side; see "Repair log (Review-repair iteration 2)"); **patched 2026-10-09 in review-repair iteration 3** (focused re-review iteration 3 found 2 architecture blockers, NB3 and NB4, and 0 adversarial blockers; the operator kept Reply in scope, sequenced last; see "Repair log (Review-repair iteration 3)"; the re-review has NOT been re-run on this patch) **patched 2026-10-09 in review-repair iteration 4** (focused re-review iteration 4 found 2 architecture blockers, NB5 and NB6, and 0 adversarial blockers; see "Repair log (Review-repair iteration 4)"; the re-review has NOT been re-run on this patch) **patched 2026-10-09 in review-repair iteration 5, the last repair round** (simplification of the write-surface guard machinery, no feature or protection cut; see "Repair log (Review-repair iteration 5 - simplification)"; the re-review has NOT been re-run on this patch)

the whole-tree scan was removed in review-repair iteration 5 and is a deferred upgrade), with two audited UI exceptions (Reply, and the O7 backlog steer of a live review session, O7 confirmed by the operator), token-gated steer writers (`steerUnderLease` and the lease-consuming `steerAuthorized`, reachable from handlers only with a `steerAuthorization` built by the access-decision block and constructed only in one file), the `acquirer` rows of the pinned-caller table for internal writers, the pinned callers of `SteerActiveSession` (backed by a behavioral test), and the per-instance write lease as a passed `*HeldLease` with one acquirer per call chain (proved at runtime, no static nesting rule); ADR-006 tray non-modal overlay; ADR-007 evolve in-repo toast stack; ADR-008 bulk actions scoped to server-computed `is_pending_decision` (also stamped on the live event; an auto-remediating WARNING is not a pending decision; the toast deck has one bulk control, "Move all to tray"); ADR-009 mobile toast stack docks at the top of the session page, desktop stays bottom-right (operator decision; device checks gate enabling the flag, not the merge); ADR-010 audited Reply to a pending question in a hidden session (operator decision O2; kept in scope, sequenced last, residual risk stated). (All in `project_plans/notification-tray-and-hidden-session-gate/decisions/`.)

## Creative Pass (alternatives considered before committing)

**Backend gate**
- A. Producer-side checks at each `NewNotificationEvent` call site. Strength: no bus change. Weakness: 47 call sites (non-test, recounted by `grep` at `013856269`), misses the `EventSessionUpdated` push, is the pattern that regressed three times.
- B. **EventBus publish filter + one shared pure policy function, also called from the 3 non-bus paths (chosen).** Strength: zero producer edits, drops before `Seq` so replay cannot resurrect. Weakness: the filter runs on the caller goroutine of ~108 `Publish` sites (recounted), so it must be an in-memory read only (VisibilityIndex + atomic flag), never poller, DB or config I/O.
- C. Every subscriber calls a shared helper. Strength: no bus coupling. Weakness: a fourth subscriber silently bypasses it.

**Toast stack**
- A. Adopt `sonner`. Strength: stacking, swipe, pause for free. Weakness: second queue duplicates `NotificationContext` state and has no pinned/never-clear-decision model.
- B. **Extract and evolve the in-repo stack (chosen).** Strength: one source of truth, policy is the hard part and no library models it. Weakness: we own timer code (small, unit-testable).
- C. CSS-only shrink. Strength: trivial. Weakness: no bulk control (rejected in requirements).

**Tray**
- A. New standalone tray component. Strength: clean slate. Weakness: duplicates history, search, filter, auto-handled section.
- B. **Evolve `NotificationPanel` into a non-modal overlay (chosen).** Strength: already a fixed sibling of `<main>`; only semantics/variants change. Weakness: file is 286 lines and already carries the modal assumptions.
- C. Docked push-aside panel. Strength: no overlap. Weakness: resizes the terminal (rejected).

## Domain Glossary
*(Exact names are used in code, tests and comments.)*

| Term | Definition | Notes |
|------|-----------|-------|
| `HiddenSession` | A session whose `Snapshot().Hidden` is true (review, triage, diagnose, headless) | Read only via `Snapshot()` per `.claude/rules/instance-lock-free-reads.md` |
| `Visibility` | Index result: `VisibilityVisible`, `VisibilityHidden`, `VisibilityNotASession`, `VisibilityUnresolved` | Typed int enum, not `bool`; only `Hidden` is gated; `Unresolved` fails open. Resolution order: the event's `SessionID` against the index, then the `item_id` metadata value against the index (the PermanentlyFailed notification is published via `Notify(inst.UUID, ...)`, which stamps `item_id=<session UUID>`: `session/session_driver.go:1004`, `server/services/backlog_notifier.go:30`), then `NotASession` rules. `NotASession` is positive-only: empty ID, an `item_id` that is not a known session, or an ID in the closed system-ID set (Task 2.2d). Everything else not found is `Unresolved` |
| `DeliveryClass` | `ClassNeedsHuman`, `ClassFailure`, `ClassRoutine` | Typed enum with exhaustive `switch` test over every `sessionv1.NotificationType` |
| `Outcome` | `OutcomeDeliver`, `OutcomeSuppress`, `OutcomeShadowSuppress` (typed enum) | `ShadowSuppress` = policy says suppress but the flag is off, so the Gate delivers and counts. `{Deliver:false, Shadow:true}` is unrepresentable |
| `Decision` | `{Outcome Outcome; Class DeliveryClass; Reason Reason}` returned by `ShouldDeliver` | `Reason` is a typed string enum with a closed set |
| `ClassHint` | `HintNone`, `HintFailure`, `HintRoutine`, `HintUntrustedType`; parsed once from `delivery_class` metadata at the filter boundary | Replaces the stringly-typed `classOverride`. `HintFailure` promotes a hard-stop WARNING; `HintRoutine` demotes a per-tool hook ERROR for hidden sessions; `HintUntrustedType` is stamped server-side by `SendNotification` on a request without `ssq_notify_schema` (an old script's type numbers collide) and makes a hidden session's event deliver (fail open, counted) |
| `Facts` | `{Type sessionv1.NotificationType; Hint ClassHint}` | The only input besides `Visibility`; no `priority` (no rule uses it), no raw `int32` pair to swap, and no status field: a crash is published as a FAILURE notification (Story 2.10), so the policy needs no status concept |
| `ShouldDeliver` | Pure function `(Visibility, Facts) Decision` | Single policy; lives in `server/deliverygate/policy.go`; uses `sessionv1` constants directly (already imported for the exhaustiveness test; `sessionv1` does not import `server/services`, so no cycle and no second drift surface) |
| `Gate` | Concrete type that **owns** the `VisibilityIndex`, `FlagCache` and counters from construction (there is no later `Bind`). Implements the small consumer-side interfaces below; no consumer receives the whole `Gate` | `server/deliverygate/gate.go`. Created and installed as the bus filter at both `NewEventBus` sites; `SeedFromInstances` runs synchronously in `BuildRuntimeDeps` right after `storage.LoadInstances()` (`server/dependencies.go:652`), before any instance is wired or started |
| Consumer-side gates | `push.SessionDeliveryGate { AllowStatusChange(sessionID string) bool }`; `services.AutoApprovedGate { AllowAutoApprovedRow(sessionID, decision string) bool }` (declared next to the handler in `server/services/approval_handler.go`, which is `package services`; there is no `approval` package) | One method each, declared in the consuming package; fakes stay one line. Only `server/push` is guaranteed not to import `server/deliverygate`; `services` constructs the Gate. There is no `EvaluateReviewItem` (Story 2.9 deletes the RQM predicate instead) |
| `PublishFilter` | `func(*events.Event) bool` from `Gate.PublishFilter()`, installed at bus construction; the Gate owns its index, seeded synchronously in `BuildRuntimeDeps` | Applies to `EventNotification` only; others pass |
| `VisibilityIndex` | Copy-on-write in-memory map: an `atomic.Pointer` to an immutable struct of session UUID, title and tmux name to `{Visibility, Kind}`, plus a bounded tombstone set for deleted hidden sessions | Reads are lock-free atomic loads. Writers (create/update/rename/delete feed points, seed, refresh) take a private writer-only mutex that guards copy-and-swap and calls nothing, so an `Upsert` under `inst.mu` cannot invert. Fed synchronously from session lifecycle (primary feed: `CreateDirectorySession`), seeded synchronously from the loaded instances, reconciled by an async refresh. Caches positive results only |
| `SessionVisibilityResolver` | Read port over `VisibilityIndex` used by the filter and the consumer-side gates; returns `{Visibility, UUID, Title, Kind}`. A miss returns `Unresolved` and schedules an async singleflight refresh with a bounded timeout **and a 5s minimum interval between attempts** (success or failure) | Never does synchronous I/O. The `SendNotification` RPC keeps its own existing poller/storage lookup for name and UUID (Task 2.2e) |
| `FlagCache` | One immutable snapshot `{global bool; kindOverrides map[HiddenKind]bool}` behind an `atomic.Pointer`, swapped only by `Reload()`: it takes a `reloadMu`, loads the config **inside** the lock and swaps. The 5s ticker (stopped and joined on server shutdown) and `UpdateFeatureFlag` (after its save, under `updateMu`) both call it, so a stale ticker read cannot overwrite an operator rollback; `FeatureFlagService` notifies it through a one-method `FlagObserver.OnFlagChanged(name)` | Replaces `config.LoadConfig()` on the publish path. `EnabledFor(kind)` applies the scope precedence (Story 2.11) with no I/O. A reload error keeps the last good snapshot (a read error never flips the gate) |
| `TerminalAccess` | Typed `ReadOnly`/`ReadWrite`, produced only by `AccessFor(inst)` from `inst.Snapshot().Hidden`. `access.Writer()` returns a `TerminalWriter` capability (an error for `ReadOnly`) | Every non-MCP terminal-write helper and every params struct on a UI write path holds a `TerminalWriter`, not a `TerminalAccess` or a `readOnly` bool, so an ignored check cannot compile into a write (Story 5.1d). `access.QuestionReplyWriter(claim)` (ADR-010) and `access.BacklogSteerWriter(link)` (ADR-005, operator decision O7) are the **two UI exceptions**; `steerAuthorized` is a third, token-gated class and the internal non-RPC writers are the `acquirer` rows of the short pinned-caller table (ADR-005 decision 3), so "two" is a statement about UI exceptions only. `AccessFor(inst)` is the constructor for stream sites; `AccessForUnary(inst, flag GuardsFlag)` is the only other one, used only by the Story 5.2 unary handlers so the `hidden_session_readonly_guards` flag can open them (the pinned-caller test asserts stream sites never call it) |
| `is_pending_decision` | Server-sent bool, additive, on **both** `NotificationHistoryRecord` (`IsActionableType(type) && !is_read`) and the live `NotificationEvent` (`events.proto`, `IsActionableType(type)` at creation, stamped in `server/services/event_converter.go:72`) | The single source of truth for bulk actions and pinning; the client never recomputes it (ADR-008). A live toast and its later history record therefore agree by construction |
| `DeliveryChannel` | `bus`, `push_status`, `auto_approved`, `slack` label values on suppression logs/counters | Label set is closed |
| `delivery_class` (metadata key) | Optional producer stamp, value `failure`; lets a WARNING from a hard-stop producer pass for a hidden session | `events.MetadataKeyDeliveryClass` in `pkg/events/notification_metadata.go` |
| `HiddenSessionGateFlag` | Server feature flag `hidden_session_gate`; off = behavior exactly as today plus shadow counters | Global value plus per-`HiddenKind` overrides (Story 2.11, ADR-004). Legacy checks stay until the default-on commit |
| `TrayV2Flag` | Web feature flag `notification_tray_v2`; off = legacy toast list + modal panel | Default off |
| `ReadOnlyStream` | A terminal stream attached to a `HiddenSession`; drops `Input` and `Resize` frames | Server-decided; `acceptsInput()` helper |
| `BackgroundActivity` | Tray section listing hidden sessions: failures/needs-human as rows, routine completions as a count | Fed by `ListSessions{hidden_only:true}` |
| `ToastStack` | Component owning visible-toast layout, cap, "+N more" chip, deck header | Extracted from `NotificationContext.tsx` |
| `ToastTimerRegistry` | `Map<id, timeoutId>` with pause/resume/cancel, owned by `ToastStack` | The only timer owner; `NotificationToast.tsx` becomes presentational (its auto-close/minimize `useEffect` at L99-135 moves into the stack) |
| `PinnedToast` | A toast whose live event has `is_pending_decision == true` (server-stamped on `NotificationEvent`), or any toast whose merged history record has it. WARNING-type events from an auto-remediating producer are **not** pinned (see `PendingDecision`) | **Presentational only**, never used to decide deletion. There is no client type list: `isPinned(toast)` reads the field. Story 3.2 states the same rule |
| `InformationalNotification` | A history row with `is_pending_decision == false` (this includes an auto-remediating WARNING such as "PR needs attention ... An automated fix attempt will run") | Target of Clear informational |
| `AutoRemediating` | Metadata stamp `auto_remediating=true` (`events.MetadataKeyAutoRemediating`) set by a producer whose WARNING only reports that automation is already acting; known producer `session/backlog_lifecycle_pr.go:1317` (`l.notify(..., 8 /*WARNING*/, false, true)`, text "An automated fix attempt will run on the standard backoff schedule") | Makes that event non-pinned (toast auto-closes, row is dismissible and clearable). If the automation fails the producer already escalates with a new ERROR/FAILURE, which pins. Candidate second producer `server/services/autonomous_orchestration_service.go:679` ("an automated turn-budget respawn attempt failed ... will retry automatically"): type UNVERIFIED, classified by Spike 1.3c |
| `PendingDecision` | A row or live event with `is_pending_decision == true` (server: `IsPendingDecision(type, metadata, read)` = `IsActionableType(type)` (`server/notifications/store.go:55`) AND the producer did not stamp `auto_remediating=true`, plus unread for records). The tray group that shows them is **"Needs attention"** (renamed from "Needs a decision": the set includes errors and failures, not only approvals) | One definition, server-computed, shared by the pin, the bulk guard (`ClearByIDs`, `Clear`) and the prune. Client `ACTIONABLE_TYPES`/`isActionableNotification`, `isActionable` and the proposed `isInformational` are removed or reduced to reading this field (Story 3.2) |
| `OverflowChip` | "+N more" button that opens the tray | `data-testid="toast-overflow-chip"` |
| `Tray` | The evolved `NotificationPanel` as a non-modal overlay | One component, `TrayVariant` layout prop |
| `TrayVariant` | First: `side-overlay`, `bottom-sheet`. Last in Epic 4 (Task 4.2e): `top-sheet`, `landscape-panel` | Picked from breakpoint (until Task 4.2e lands, the bottom sheet height follows `--viewport-height`, so the soft keyboard needs no extra variant) |
| `TrayHandle` | Persistent edge handle (desktop) / chip (mobile) with capped unread + pinned count | Count capped at `99+` |
| `QuietMode` | In scope, sequenced last in Epic 4 (Story 4.5). Per-device toggle demoting every non-pinned toast to the tray | Persisted in `localStorage` with try/catch |
| `SwipeToDismiss` | `useSwipeToDismiss` hook, row-scoped, horizontal axis lock | Always paired with a 44px button |
| `NOTIFICATIONS_BULK_DISMISSED` | BroadcastChannel message `{ ids: string[] }` | Idempotent id set (ADR-008) |
| `ClearByIDs` | `NotificationHistoryStore` method deleting listed ids except unread actionable records; returns `(deleted, kept []string)` | Server safety net; the client restores `kept` rows it removed optimistically |
| `HiddenKind` | `review`, `triage`, `diagnose`, `other`: the kind of a hidden session, derived at index upsert and **re-derived on every tag change** from `Snapshot().Tags` (`backlog:review`, `backlog:triage`, `backlog:diagnose`; any other hidden session is `other`). Tags are mutable and may co-occur, so the order is total: `review` > `diagnose` > `triage` > `other` | The only flag scope in v1 (Story 2.11). No producer creates a `backlog:triage` Instance (the literal is only read, `session/session_driver.go:1112`), so `kind:triage` is shown and accepted only if Spike 1.3h proves it reachable. A kind is a flag-scope key only: the O7 steer eligibility never reads it (Task 5.2e keys on the durable `item_session` row) |
| `hidden_only` | New `ListSessionsRequest` bool (field 9); returns only hidden sessions | Requires `include_hidden` semantics internally |
| `PendingQuestion` | In-memory record created when an `AskUserQuestion` permission request fires (`approval_handler.go:424`) **and the session was resolved from the `X-CS-Session-ID` header to a live instance** (a cwd-prefix or unknown attribution never registers one): `{QuestionID (128 random bits), SessionUUID, PromptText, QuestionToken (derived from `tool_input`: the normalized first 40 runes of the question text, else the first option label; none means not replyable), QuestionShape (single/multi/unknown, from Spike 1.3g), State (candidate, confirmed, claimed, claimed-superseded, consumed, superseded, expired), Fingerprint, CreatedAt}`; keyed by UUID; bounded (256 total, 8 per session) with a TTL | The precondition for Reply. It is a `candidate` until the detector reports a status in the set recorded by Spike 1.3g **within a window of seconds from the hook** and the live pane fingerprint (whose region must contain the `QuestionToken`) is captured; a newer question, **or any other dialog-producing hook for the session (every `PermissionRequest`, an unattributed question matching a live instance)**, supersedes older ids. The notification carries `question_id` and `question_shape` in metadata |
| `PendingQuestionClaim` | Single-use token returned only by `PendingQuestionStore.Claim(session, questionID, replyID)` (one critical section; requires `inst.UUID == pending.SessionUUID`); unexported fields | The capability that `access.QuestionReplyWriter` requires; no claim, no write (ADR-010). Released only for `NOT_SENT` (failure strictly before the first byte, proven by a zero-byte count), and a released claim is re-validated (expiry, newest id, still hidden) before it is claimable again; a supersede or session delete that arrives while it is `claimed` marks it `claimed-superseded`, so a later release returns it to `superseded`, never `confirmed`; consumed by `SENT` and `SEND_INDETERMINATE` |
| `ReplyToPendingQuestion` | Typed RPC: answers the one question on screen in one hidden session with one single-line reply (1..500 **bytes**); audited (no reply text in the audit file or any log), rate-limited, idempotent by `reply_id` for `SENT` and `SEND_INDETERMINATE` (a repeated id while in flight, or inside the 5s limiter window, returns the first result, never `RATE_LIMITED`; `reply_id` is bound to the SHA-256 of `(session UUID, question_id, text)` and a mismatch is `InvalidArgument`; the result cache is bounded); expected outcomes are a `ReplyOutcome` enum in the response, Connect errors only for `InvalidArgument`, `Internal`, `PermissionDenied`, `Unauthenticated` | One of the two UI writes into a hidden session (the other is the O7 backlog steer); explicitly allowlisted in the Story 5.1d scan |
| `ReplyOutcome` | Response enum of `ReplyToPendingQuestion`, spelled with the `ENUM_VALUE_PREFIX` the repo's `STANDARD` buf lint requires: `REPLY_OUTCOME_UNSPECIFIED = 0`, `REPLY_OUTCOME_SENT`, `REPLY_OUTCOME_SEND_INDETERMINATE`, `REPLY_OUTCOME_NOT_SENT`, `REPLY_OUTCOME_NO_PENDING`, `REPLY_OUTCOME_SUPERSEDED`, `REPLY_OUTCOME_STALE_PROMPT`, `REPLY_OUTCOME_NOT_HIDDEN`, `REPLY_OUTCOME_NOT_WAITING`, `REPLY_OUTCOME_RATE_LIMITED`, `REPLY_OUTCOME_DISABLED` (plus `retry_after_seconds`); this text abbreviates them as `SENT`, `NOT_SENT` and so on. **There is no `IN_PROGRESS`** (review-repair iteration 2, ARCH-C21): a second caller with the same `reply_id` waits for and returns the first result, and a busy write lease is `NOT_SENT` | Added to Contract PR 3 before it merges. Metric labels are the abbreviated names lower-cased plus `invalid`, `audit_failed`, `refused`. `SEND_INDETERMINATE` has no Retry in the client |
| `SubmitReplyOnce` | The Reply-specific send primitive (`session/pane_reply.go`, Task 5.6k): ctx check, `VerifyPaneOwner`, the per-instance `TerminalWriteLease`, before-first-byte checks, **one** content write, settle wait, **one** Enter write only when the recorded `ReplyKeystrokes` mode has one, **no** blind Enter retry; each write goes through the count-returning `Instance.SendKeysN` and runs under its own timeout; a compare-and-swap state word (`idle`, `writing`, `writing-aborted`, `aborted`) guarantees that after the caller times out at most the one write already inside the syscall can still land |
| `ReplyKeystrokes` | Typed value chosen from Spike 1.3g's recorded dialog behavior: `ContentThenEnter` (typed text) or `DigitOnly` (a digit key selects immediately, so **no** Enter is written, because a stray Enter would pick the default of whatever follows) | `SubmitReplyOnce(ctx, inst, keys ReplyKeystrokes, beforeFirstByte)`; the reply validator for the recorded mode (free text, or one option digit) is part of the spike output |
| `TerminalWriteLease` / `HeldLease` | Per-instance exclusive, non-reentrant write lease (`session/instance_write_lease.go`, Story 5.0, own PR 5a). Acquired by `Instance.TryTerminalWriteLease` or `AcquireTerminalWriteLease`, which return a `*HeldLease` capability (unexported fields; nil, zero and foreign leases are refused with 0 writes; `ErrLeaseBusy` when held). **Exactly one function per call chain acquires** (the outermost writer); the write primitives (`SubmitDriverContent`, `SubmitContentWithEnter`, `SendKeysWithTimeout`, `SubmitReplyOnce`, `steerAuthorized`) only **receive** it, and the **innermost function that issues the write releases it exactly once on every return path, in the goroutine that issues the write**, never the caller returning | Stops a driver `"1\n"` (`session/session_driver.go:365`) or nudge from landing between a steer's or reply's content and its Enter and stops an abandoned blocked write from overlapping the next writer. Busy: the driver skips the tick without latching (a busy lease is not a latch attempt, and the initial prompt consumes no attempt), a nudge, MCP tool or steer returns a retryable error, a Reply is `NOT_SENT` (`Try`, no wait). Nesting is caught at runtime by the real chain tests (T-RO-43) and the nesting fixture (T-WL-02), and the acquirer list is closed and pinned (T-RO-45); there is **no** static nesting rule, **no** `ErrNestedLease` and no context marker. A wedged write holds the lease (gauge, WARN, one tray-level warning per episode; remedy: Pause or Delete; no forced release). Live-settable flag `terminal_write_lease` (default on; off = a non-exclusive lease). The lease is a passed `*HeldLease`, so a primitive called without one does not compile; the capability-literal lint and the pinned acquirer list (Story 5.1d) cover the same-package gap |
| `FlagMutation` | `UpdateFeatureFlagRequest.mutation` enum, spelled with the `ENUM_VALUE_PREFIX` the `STANDARD` buf lint requires: `FLAG_MUTATION_UNSPECIFIED = 0` (the legacy shape an old client sends: `scope` empty, `enabled` read; **UNSPECIFIED with a non-empty `scope` is `InvalidArgument`**), `FLAG_MUTATION_SET_ENABLED = 1`, `FLAG_MUTATION_SET_DISABLED = 2`, `FLAG_MUTATION_CLEAR_SCOPE = 3`, `FLAG_MUTATION_RESET_GLOBAL = 4`; this text abbreviates them `SET_ENABLED`, `SET_DISABLED`, `CLEAR_SCOPE`, `RESET_GLOBAL`. An unknown enum value is `InvalidArgument` | Replaces the earlier `bool clear_override`. The enum carries the value of a set (so a `SET` cannot write `false` because the plain `bool enabled` was omitted; `enabled` is ignored for every non-UNSPECIFIED mutation); a set takes `scope = "global"` (a literal) or `"kind:<kind>"` and **an empty `scope` on a set is `InvalidArgument`**, so a client that drops `scope` cannot write the global value; `CLEAR_SCOPE` needs a `kind:` scope and `RESET_GLOBAL` forbids a scope (ADR-004 decision 4) |
| `LocalWriteGuard` | `middleware.ProbeGuard` generalized (Story 1.8: **PR 1f-a** stamps the request record and merges before PR 2a-2; **PR 1f-b** adds the verdict, the set and the verified-hostname set and merges before PR 2c and PR 5u) to a set of guarded procedure paths on the unauthenticated `:8543` listener, plus one **peer-aware verdict** function that the in-handler checks also call. Verdict profiles: `ProbeProgram` keeps today's exact rule (POST, loopback-bound listener, Host and Origin loopback or allowed, `GetHostnames()`); the **rebinding gate** (steer, Prune, Reply) requires Host in {loopback names, `GetVerifiedHostnames()` (the names for which `validateFn` returned true in the latest detector cycle: recomputed each cycle from the candidates, seeded at boot from the boot-time `verifiedHostnames`, never copied from `d.networks`), a local IP literal, the host of a non-wildcard listen address} and Origin, if present, loopback, in `GetOrigins()` or same-origin, with **no** `LoopbackBound` condition, and **skips the Host test when `auth_mode == required`** (`:8444`); the **local-caller gate** (Reply only) additionally refuses when auth is off and the caller is remote (peer not loopback, or any proxy header present, reusing `isProxyHeader`). Both chains stamp a request record `{listener, auth_mode, host, origin, proxied}` through a sibling of `services.WithRequestHost` (`server/server.go:430`), with `auth_mode == required` only when a non-nil validator is wired (`requiresAuth`, `main.go:1614`) | Guarded set: `ProbeProgram` (Task 1.8b), `PruneHiddenSessionNotifications` (whole procedure, dry run included; Task 2.7c), `ReplyToPendingQuestion` (Task 5.6l); `ClearNotificationHistory` is not added; the set is not installed when an `authMiddleware` is set. A safety rail, not a security boundary (ADR-010 decision 7) |
| `steerUnderLease` / `steerAuthorized` / `steerInternal` / `decideSteerAccess` | `steerUnderLease(ctx, auth, inst, msg)` is the visible-target acquirer that `UpdateSession` calls; `steerInternal(ctx, inst, msg)` is the thin internal acquirer for `SteerActiveSession` and the only other constructor of a token (kind `internal`); both take the lease once and call `steerAuthorized(ctx, auth steerAuthorization, lease *HeldLease, inst, msg)`, the token-gated steer writer (the former `steerInstance` body), which **never acquires** and refuses a token built for another instance, the zero-value token and a foreign lease. `decideSteerAccess(...)` is the **only** function that may construct a visible-target or link token (`UpdateSession`'s access-decision block calls it) | The capability-literal lint (Story 5.1d check (a)) keeps every construction of the token type in one file, `steer_authorization.go`, which holds `decideSteerAccess` and the `internal`-kind constructor; the pinned-caller test (check (c)) pins the callers of `steerUnderLease`, `steerInternal`, `steerAuthorized` and `SteerActiveSession` (the PR-fix and verdict steers are pinned by a behavioral test); `steerAuthorized` refuses a mismatched or zero token at runtime. The compiler alone cannot enforce construction inside package `services`, and no scan rule checks that a handler passes a token from `decideSteerAccess` (T-RO-37 covers it at runtime) |
| `steerAuthorization` | Unexported token `{kind (visible, backlog_link, internal), instanceUUID}` passed into `steerUnderLease` and `steerAuthorized`; construction is confined to one defining file by the capability-literal lint (see the row above), and the zero value is refused at runtime | The lint, not the compiler, forbids construction outside `steer_authorization.go` by literal, `var`, `new` or conversion (ADR-005 decisions 3 and 5) |
| `GuardsFlag` | Interface `ReadOnlyGuardsEnabled() bool` backed by an atomic the flag service updates; injected into `AccessForUnary`; fails closed (unreadable config keeps the guards on) | Replaces a `guardsEnabled bool` parameter that any caller could pass as `true` |
| `StatsStore` | Small interface in `server/deliverygate` (`Load() (PersistedStats, error)`, `Save(PersistedStats) error`) with a no-op default; the file implementation lives in `server/services/gatestats_store.go` and receives the config directory once at construction | Keeps file I/O, `fsync` and the writer goroutine out of the pure policy package; the writer is owned by the server (`backgroundTasksWG`) |
| `TrayEntryChip` / `TrayAffordance` | One stable phone tray entry (ux.md D10): a single mounted node on a session page whose content is `bell`, `more-row` or `keyboard-chip`; `floating-bottom` only on a page with no terminal (Task 4.2i) | Prevents a relocating entry (flicker, relocation race) and two entries reading as duplicates |
| `BacklogReviewLink` | Value returned only by `BacklogLinkResolver.ResolveLiveReviewLink(sessionUUID)`; predicate on **durable state**: the newest `item_session` row for the UUID (`GetItemSessionBySessionUUID` returns the newest row of any role, `session/storage_backlog.go:380`) has `Role == SessionRoleReview` and `EndedAt == nil` (as `IsDiagnoseCaller`, `session/storage.go:1358`), **and** the instance is live (status not Stopped, Crashed or Paused, `VerifyPaneOwner` passes), re-checked after the audit append; unexported fields | The capability behind the O7 backlog-steer path: no link, no write. The mutable `backlog:review` tag is not part of the predicate (a tag edit never changes eligibility). The predicate is pinned by Task 5.2d |
| `hidden_session_readonly_guards` | Server feature flag, default on, global only (not scopable), live-settable via `UpdateFeatureFlag`; read through an injected `GuardsFlag`, fails closed | Turns the Story 5.2 unary guards (`WriteToSession`, the steer branch's hidden rejection, `RestartSession`/`RestartShell`) off for an incident without a deploy; the stream drops (Story 5.1) are not affected, and it does **not** gate Reply. While off, the O7 path stays active and audited, and a steer to a non-qualifying hidden target is allowed only with an audit line `kind=guard_bypass` and a rate-limited WARN. Every flip is audited; a safety rail, not a security boundary |
| `hidden_session_reply` | Server feature flag, default **on** (kill-switch semantics), global only, live-settable via `UpdateFeatureFlag` (Task 5.6j); fails closed (unreadable config reads as off) | Off: `ReplyToPendingQuestion` returns outcome `DISABLED` (0 writes, claim not taken) and the client hides the Reply card and action (View output remains). Independent of `hidden_session_readonly_guards`, so rolling back Reply never re-opens the unary guards and vice versa. Turning it on is audited before it is persisted |
| `Announcer` | The single provider-level owner of live regions: one always-mounted polite region and one always-mounted assertive region, fed by `announce(message, politeness, key?)` with a priority queue and burst coalescing (Story 3.6) | No notification surface (toast, deck, tray, banner, Reply card, kept line) renders its own `role=status` or `role=alert`; they call `announce()`. Built on the existing `web-app/src/components/ui/LiveRegion.tsx` primitive (`role` prop) |
| `UndoWindow` | Per-device configurable undo duration (default 8s, options 5/8/15/30s), paused while the undo control is hovered or focused, shared by every undo toast and tray undo bar (Task 4.4g) | Deferred destructive RPC is sent when the window ends or the page is hidden (Task 4.4g) |
| `ConnectivityState` | `online` iff `selectConnectionState` (`web-app/src/lib/store/sessionsSlice`, the store behind `ConnectionIndicator.tsx:5-6`) is `connected`; `stale` and `disconnected` are `offline`. A `GetNotificationHistory` failure while connected is a separate `load-error` state | The one connectivity source for the toast deck, tray, Background and Reply card (Task 4.3e) |
| `DeliveryGateStats` | In-process mirror of every gate counter (full labels, since process start) plus **72 persisted hourly buckets** carrying `gate_on_seconds` (global and per kind), `uptime_seconds`, `hidden_events_seen`, `routine_events_while_on` and reduced counters keyed `(counter, kind, class)`; a short flag-change history; `process_start`; and a server-computed **`soak_streak_hours`** (formerly `soak_gate_on_hours_with_traffic`; reshaped in review-repair iterations 2 and 3: gate-on hours counted from the last off flip, only in buckets with events the gate decided to suppress, excluding the flip bucket, plus `stats_file_status.writable`, `soak.failure_delivered_while_on >= 1` and an empty `soak.explicit_off_scopes` as Stage 3 conditions). Persisted by the `StatsStore` file adapter to `<config dir>/delivery-gate-stats.json` (atomic write, versioned schema, SHA-256 checksum, corruption recovery; Task 2.8f); returned by `GetDeliveryGateStats` | Exists because `telemetry.GetMeter()` is a no-op meter when telemetry is not initialized (`telemetry/telemetry.go:274-279`), so the Stage 2 soak cannot depend on OTel (Story 2.8). Reachable on `:8543` without auth and on `:8444` behind auth; exposes aggregate counters only (no session id, title or reply text) |
| `PruneHiddenSessionNotifications` | Operator-triggered admin RPC; `apply=false` (dry run) by default; behind the auth middleware on `:8444`, and on `:8543` (no auth: `SetupAuth`, `server/server.go:1321`, has no non-test caller) **in the `LocalWriteGuard` procedure set, whole procedure, rebinding gate** (review-repair iteration 2: it is a destructive bulk delete introduced by this project; `ClearNotificationHistory`, with the same exposure today, is **not** widened here); an apply appends a `kind=prune` audit line before it deletes; not exposed over MCP | Replaces the restart-time one-shot cleanup (Story 2.7). Backup file name carries a UTC timestamp |

## Pattern Decisions

| Component | Pattern Chosen | Source | Alternative Rejected | Reason |
|-----------|---------------|--------|---------------------|--------|
| Delivery policy (`ShouldDeliver`) | Pure function / Transaction Script over value inputs | Fowler PoEAA (Transaction Script) | Domain Model with notification entities | Stateless rule table; nothing to model |
| `Gate` | Decorator over `EventBus.Publish` (filter hook) | GoF Decorator / Chain of Responsibility (single link) | Per-subscriber checks | A new subscriber would bypass it |
| `SessionVisibilityResolver` | Adapter + Repository-style read port | GoF Adapter, Fowler Repository | Calling poller/storage inline | Isolates poller/storage and caches; lets tests fake it |
| `Visibility` | Sum type (typed int enum, exhaustive switch) | type-driven-design | `hidden bool` | `Unresolved` must not collapse into `Visible` silently |
| `DeliveryClass` | Sum type + exhaustiveness test over proto enum | type-driven-design | priority number | Priority already proved unreliable (ceiling rows) |
| Session IDs at the gate | Keep `string`; item-ID vs session-ID discriminated by resolver result | type-driven-design | New `SessionID` newtype across 47 producers | Out of proportion; resolver result carries the distinction |
| Read-only stream | A `TerminalWriter` capability on the params struct (nil for a read-only attach) + shared `acceptsInput()` | Fowler (Replace Conditional with guard) + capability-based design | Per-handler `if hidden`; a `readOnly bool` field | `dupl` gate and drift; a bool can be ignored, a missing capability cannot compile into a write |
| Toast timers | Registry (Map of handles) owned by `ToastStack` only; `NotificationToast` is presentational | GoF Command-ish, React ref registry | Two timer owners (provider registry + per-toast `useEffect`) | Pause-on-hover and pinned-never-expire must reach every timer; one owner is the only way to guarantee it |
| Stack policy | Pure reducer-style functions (`partitionToasts`) | Fowler Transaction Script | Logic in component | Deterministic unit tests, no fake DOM |
| Tray layout variants | Strategy keyed by `TrayVariant` | GoF Strategy | Two components | jscpd gate; one panel |
| Swipe | Custom hook modeled on `useWindowSwipe.ts` | repo precedent | `@use-gesture/react` | Reuses tested thresholds; fallback documented |
| Bulk dismissal sync | Idempotent id-set message | event-sourcing style idempotent command | "clear now" message | Would delete items that arrived after the click |
| Feature gating | `FlagCache`: one immutable `{global, per-HiddenKind overrides}` snapshot behind an `atomic.Pointer`, swapped only by a mutex-guarded `Reload()` that the 5s ticker and `UpdateFeatureFlag` both call; web flag via `useFeatureFlags()` | `config.LoadConfig()` reads and parses the file on every call (`config/config.go:1373`), so it cannot run per `Publish`; `ProgramCLIFlagProbeEnabled` is a per-RPC precedent only | per-call `LoadConfig`, env var; two independent swappers | Must be live-settable, scoped (O1), AND free of I/O on ~108 `Publish` call sites; one reload path stops a stale ticker read from undoing a rollback |
| Visibility lookup | Copy-on-write in-memory index (`atomic.Pointer` to an immutable map) fed by lifecycle events and synchronously seeded from the loaded instances; positive results only; async singleflight refresh on miss with a 5s minimum interval | Fowler Repository (read model), CQRS-style read side; `golang-concurrency` skill (atomic COW for read-mostly state) | Sync `ListInstanceData` / poller `FindInstance` on the publisher goroutine; an `RWMutex` index | `FindInstance` takes `rqp.mu.RLock` and `MatchesID` takes `pmMu.Lock` per instance (`session/instance_tmux.go:316`); `Publish` callers include lock-holding lifecycle callbacks; an atomic-pointer index makes "lock-free read" literally true and `Upsert` under `inst.mu` safe (private writer mutex, nothing called while held). Writes are rare (session create/rename/delete, tens per day), so copying a map of a few hundred sessions is cheap (INFERRED, measured by a benchmark in Task 2.2b) |
| Terminal write access | Parse, don't validate: `TerminalAccess` resolved once from `Snapshot().Hidden`, yielding a `TerminalWriter` capability that every UI write helper requires; plus four checks (literal lints, the RPC-descriptor classification, a short pinned-caller table of the pane-typing primitives and a presence-AND-use rule for stream writers) | type-driven-design | `if hidden` at each call site; a whole-tree five-class scan (built in iterations 3-4, removed in iteration 5: the capability made it largely redundant and each review found new defects in it); a scan that only checks a parameter exists | The write surface is wider than the 4 stream sites; the capability proves "forgot the guard" at compile time and the three checks cover the gaps the compiler cannot see (a new RPC, a new caller, same-package construction) |
| Reply write path | Capability token: `PendingQuestionStore.Claim` returns a single-use `PendingQuestionClaim`; `access.QuestionReplyWriter(claim)` is the only write for a `ReadOnly` target and its method is `SubmitReplyOnce`, a Reply-specific one-content-write, one-Enter, no-retry primitive; claim bound to the dialog on screen (supersede on every dialog-producing hook, candidate-until-seen within seconds, live pane fingerprint whose region contains the question token) and serialized against the session driver and nudges by a per-instance write lease | type-driven-design (parse, don't validate), ADR-010 | Re-opening `steer` for hidden sessions; reusing `SubmitContentWithEnter` and releasing the claim on any error (it types twice); a boolean "allow reply" parameter | The exception must be provable and narrow: a claim exists only for the question that is on screen, and a failure after the first byte consumes the claim instead of inviting a second typing |
| Backlog steer on hidden review sessions (O7) | Capability token: `BacklogLinkResolver.ResolveLiveReviewLink` returns a `BacklogReviewLink` only for a live review session whose newest durable `item_session` row is an un-ended review row; `access.BacklogSteerWriter(link)` is the second (and last) narrow **UI** exception; the decision is made first in `UpdateSession` by `decideSteerAccess` and handed to the token-gated `steerAuthorized` as an unexported `steerAuthorization` (the internal `SteerActiveSession` uses `steerInternal`) | type-driven-design, ADR-005 | Dropping steer for hidden sessions (breaks the shipped backlog "Steer" composer, `BacklogItemDetail.tsx:628`); a hidden-kind `if` in the steer branch; a tag-based predicate | Preserves shipped behavior with a provable boundary: a hidden triage/diagnose/other, unlinked, ended or re-linked session has no link, so its steer is unrepresentable |
| Pending-decision predicate | One server-computed `is_pending_decision` field on the history record AND the live `NotificationEvent`; client reads it | Single source of truth | 4 predicates kept in parity by a fixture; a client type list for toasts | Three predicates already disagree today (WARNING); a live toast has no record yet, so the event carries the field |
