#### Story 5.2: Unary write guards
**As an** operator, **I want** UI write RPCs to reject hidden targets, **so that** no UI path writes to a hidden session.
**Gate**: Task 5.2d (the characterization test on `main`) may start now; Tasks 5.2b, 5.2e and 5.2f start only after the focused re-review PASS on item 2 ("Pending focused re-review"), **Story 5.0 (the lease), PR 1f-b (Story 1.8, the verdict that `UpdateSession` calls; it needs PR 1f-a) and PR 2a-2 (Task 2.8h, the audit sink core and the `flag_change` recording that Tasks 5.2e and 5.2f extend)** have merged (review-repair iteration 3, ARCH-C30, ADV-N20: these two edges were missing from the PR table).
**Acceptance Criteria**:
- `WriteToSession` rejects.
  - *Given* hidden session `h1`, *When* `TerminalService.WriteToSession{session_id:"h1", input:"x"}` is called, *Then* `connect.CodeFailedPrecondition`; for a visible session, success.
- Steer branch of `UpdateSession` rejects hidden targets **except the O7 backlog-steer path**; read RPCs remain open. **The access decision is made first in the handler** (review-repair iteration 1, ARCH-C4), right after the instance is found (`session_service_update.go:156-161`) and before any title, tag, category, note, working-directory or auto-approve mutation, because several of those publish in memory immediately (`:166-169`) and a steer rejected at `:315` would leave them applied. It is **not** inside the writer (`steerInstance`, `:371`), whose second caller `SteerActiveSession` (`session_service_liveness.go:96-101`) has no capability. Review-repair iterations 2 and 3 (ARCH-NB1, ARCH-NB3): `steerInstance` is **split** three ways: the visible path calls the acquirer `steerUnderLease(ctx, auth, inst, msg)` at the existing `:320` position (it takes the `TerminalWriteLease` once and hands the `*HeldLease` to the token-gated `steerAuthorized(ctx, auth, lease, inst, msg)`, which never acquires and refuses a token whose UUID is not `inst.UUID`, a zero-value token and a lease for another instance), with a token that `decideSteerAccess` (the access-decision block, the only constructor of the `visible` and `backlog_link` kinds) built for this instance UUID; the backlog path calls `access.BacklogSteerWriter(link).Steer`, which runs `steerHiddenReviewViaBacklogLink` (also an acquirer); and the thin `steerInternal(ctx, inst, msg)` that `SteerActiveSession` calls (the only other token constructor, kind `internal`) acquires and hands the lease to `steerAuthorized` as well. Story 5.1d check (a) confines token construction to `steer_authorization.go`, check (c) pins the callers of `steerInternal`, `steerUnderLease` and `steerAuthorized`, and one acquirer per chain is a design rule proved at runtime by T-RO-43 and T-WL-02.
  - *Given* a hidden target and a request that carries `steer_message` together with any other mutating field (title, tags, category, note, working dir, auto-approve, status), *Then* `InvalidArgument` (`steer_must_be_alone`) before any mutation; the composer sends only `steerMessage` (`BacklogItemDetail.tsx:628`). *Given* a rejected hidden steer, *Then* the instance's title, tags, category and note are byte-identical before and after (test).
  - *Given* hidden `h1`, *Then* `GetSession`, `GetTerminalSnapshot`, `GetSessionDiff` still succeed.
  - *Given* hidden `h1`, *When* `SteerActiveSession(h1, "go")` is called (characterization test, written first), *Then* it still succeeds exactly as before.
- **Backlog "Steer" of a hidden review session keeps working (operator decision O7, CONFIRMED 2026-10-09; pre-mortem #1).** The shipped composer calls `updateSession(s.sessionId, { steerMessage })` (`web-app/src/components/backlog/BacklogItemDetail.tsx:628`), `isSteerable` is true for `review` role sessions that have not ended (`web-app/src/lib/backlog/sessionKind.ts:52-55`) and `SpawnReviewSession` creates them `Hidden: true` (`session_service_diagnose.go:54-60`), so a blanket steer guard would break it. **The `BacklogReviewLink` predicate keys on durable state, never on a tag** (ARCH-C5, ADV C7): `GetItemSessionBySessionUUID` (`session/storage_backlog.go:380`) returns the **newest** row of **any** role, so the link exists only if that row has `Role == SessionRoleReview` and `EndedAt == nil` (`ItemSessionSummary.EndedAt`, `session/repository.go:224`; the same durable-role approach as `IsDiagnoseCaller`, `session/storage.go:1358`) **and** the instance is live (status not Stopped, Crashed or Paused, `VerifyPaneOwner` passes), re-checked after the audit append (`EndedAt` is written asynchronously, so the first check can pass on a session that just exited). The mutable `backlog:review` tag (`applyTagsUpdate`, `:60-75`, applied earlier in the same request) is not part of the predicate; a tag edit never changes steer eligibility.
  - *Given* hidden review session `r1` whose newest `item_session` row is an un-ended review row and whose instance is live, *When* the backlog composer sends `steerMessage` through `UpdateSession`, *Then* it succeeds through `access.BacklogSteerWriter(link)`, exactly one audit line (`kind=backlog_steer`, `session_uuid`, `session_title`, `item_id`, `message_len`, `message_sha256`, `message_preview` (first 80 chars; kept for the steer because it already echoes its message in `notifySteerSent`), `listener`, `peer_addr`, `host`, `origin`, `user_agent`, `auth_mode`) is appended (and `fsync`ed) to the same sink as Story 5.6 **before** the write and an outcome line after, and `hidden_session_backlog_steer_total{outcome=sent}` increments (no audit, no write; the steer text keeps the `MaxSteerMessageLength` cap of 10000 bytes **and is rejected if it contains ESC or any other control character except LF and TAB, C1, U+2028/9 or any `Cf` format character except U+200C and U+200D, i.e. the shared `ValidateSingleLineText` rules of ADR-010 decision 5 minus LF and TAB**; the steer audit keeps its 80-character `message_preview`, so the audit file is not "no message text anywhere" for steers, which `docs/reference/notification-delivery-gate.md` and `docs/how-to/manage-notifications.md` state (minor m6); the unchanged `SteerActiveSession` and visible-target steer keep today's behavior).
  - *Given* hidden review session `r2` with the `backlog:review` tag removed, or `r3` with the tag added while its newest durable row is a diagnose row, *Then* `r2` still steers and `r3` is `no_link` (the tag is irrelevant); *Given* a review session that exited but whose `EndedAt` is not yet written (the instance is Stopped, Crashed or the pane owner check fails), *Then* `no_link` with 0 writes, and a session that ends between the first check and the post-audit re-check gives outcome `no_link` with an `aborted_before_write` outcome line.
  - *Given* the Host or Origin fails the `LocalWriteGuard` **rebinding gate** (Story 1.8; the verdict is called from inside the handler through the request record that `services.WithRequestHost`'s sibling stamps), *Then* `PermissionDenied`, outcome `refused`, 0 writes, in both flag states, with a message that names the rejected Host and the remedy (the Host must be a loopback name, a local IP literal or a **verified** hostname of the server; review-repair iteration 3, ARCH-NB4: the allowlist is the forward-DNS-verified set, never `GetHostnames()`, which also holds unverified reverse-DNS names). *Given* a legitimate LAN-hostname browser (Host in the verified set, same-origin Origin) on the supported bind, including a `0.0.0.0` bind and auth off, *Then* the steer is **not** refused (regression-free: the shipped composer keeps working; ARCH-C18, ADV-N7), and the audit line records `peer_loopback=false` (and `proxied` when a proxy header is present) instead of refusing on it. *Given* the authenticated `:8444` chain (`auth_mode == required`), *Then* the Host-membership test is skipped (the auth cookie is the boundary and that chain's Host is a LAN or Tailscale name, `server/server.go:1710-1711`), the same-origin Origin check still applies, and a remote operator's Steer from `onyx.staplerhome.internal` or a Tailscale name keeps working (ADV-N14, T-RO-34). The steer deliberately does **not** apply the Reply-only local-caller gate, and the verdict has no `LoopbackBound` condition; a deployment whose browser uses a Host outside that set and has auth off is refused with the explicit message (the accepted, documented limit).
  - *Given* a hidden triage, diagnose or `other` session, an unlinked review session, or an ended review session, *When* `UpdateSession` carries a `steerMessage`, *Then* `FailedPrecondition` with 0 writes (`outcome=no_link`).
  - *Given* a visible session, *Then* steer is unchanged.
  - *Given* a visible target and an `UpdateSession` that carries `steer_message` together with other fields, *Then* the other fields are applied first and a busy lease then returns the retryable `FailedPrecondition` after that partial update; this is pre-existing for every other steer failure and is stated, not changed (only a hidden target gets `steer_must_be_alone`; review-repair iteration 4, ARCH-C36d).
- `SwitchWorkspace` rejects hidden targets, for every switch type, before any mutation (review-repair iteration 3, found while classifying the write surface; iteration 4, ARCH-C39).
  - *Given* hidden `h1`, *When* the UI `SwitchWorkspace{id:"h1", switch_type: <directory, revision or worktree>}` is called (a `SessionService` RPC that delegates to `WorkspaceService.SwitchWorkspace`, `session_service_delegates.go:249-253`; the request field is `id`, not `session_id`, `proto/session/v1/session.proto:1619-1621`), *Then* `FailedPrecondition` with 0 writes and **0 git-side mutation**: the guard runs right after `findInstanceFast` and **before** the `inFlightSwitches` store and before `CreateCheckpoint` (VERIFIED order at `server/services/workspace_service.go:341-409`: validation, the in-flight `LoadOrStore`, `findInstanceFast`, the pre-switch checkpoint at `:395-400`, `instance.SwitchWorkspace` at `:409`), so Task 5.2b moves the in-flight store to after the lookup and the guard, and a hidden target never gets a checkpoint or an in-flight entry. A directory switch types `cd "<dir>"` and a newline into the pane (`changeDirectory`, `session/instance_workspace.go:362`), and revision and worktree switches restart the agent in a new directory with `claude --resume` (`:167-229`), which ends a hidden review, so **all three types are guarded**. `WorkspaceService` has no flag today (`NewWorkspaceService(storage, eventBus)`, `workspace_service.go:85`), so the `GuardsFlag` is injected through a setter beside `SetLiveFinder` (`:91`) and read through `AccessForUnary` like `WriteToSession`; with the flag off a hidden target is allowed as on `main`; for a visible session the RPC is unchanged; T-RO-17 lists the RPC as a terminal write (T-RO-40).
- `UpdateSession`'s `program` and `auto_approve` fields reject hidden targets (review-repair iteration 4, ADV-N28; VERIFIED).
  - *Given* hidden Active `h1` and an `UpdateSession` that changes `program` or `auto_approve`, *Then* (the unguarded behavior at `013856269`) the handler reaches `Instance.SwitchProgram` (`session_service_update.go:241`) or `Instance.SetAutoApprove` (`:300`), both of which call `Restart(true)` when the instance is Active (`session/instance_program.go:102`, `session/instance_actor_setters.go:618`), and a restart with `preserveOutput` types `echo '<marker>'` and an Enter into the new pane (`session/instance.go:2608-2618`), which in a Claude pane arrives as a user prompt after the running agent was killed. The marker is fixed text, not operator or attacker content, but the earlier claim that exactly two UI paths can write to a hidden session was false, and the restart itself ends a hidden review. So **the access-decision block (first in the handler, before any mutation) also refuses these two fields for a hidden target** with `FailedPrecondition` through `AccessForUnary` (flag off: allowed as on `main`); the whole request is rejected, so nothing is partially applied; a visible target is unchanged; and `Restart` stays a `lifecycle` row whose reason is now "reached only from guarded UI entries and internal paths", with its caller set pinned (Story 5.1d, T-RO-46). `UpdateSessionProgram` (the capacity guardrail's own fallback, `capacity_monitor.go:508`) stays exempt because it is not UI-reachable. The RPC sweep found no other RPC that types into a pane; T-RO-17 lists `program` and `auto_approve` by field (T-RO-47).
- **Characterization before guard** (Task 5.2d): the test of the shipped steer to a hidden review session is written and green on `main`, **before** Task 5.2b lands; it stays as the regression test for the exemption.
- **Live escape hatch.** The unary guards (`WriteToSession`, the steer branch's hidden rejection, `RestartSession`/`RestartShell`, `SwitchWorkspace` and the `program` and `auto_approve` fields of `UpdateSession`) read the live flag `hidden_session_readonly_guards` (default on) through `AccessForUnary(inst, flag GuardsFlag)`: an injected interface backed by an atomic that the flag service updates (not a `guardsEnabled bool` parameter and not `LoadConfig()` per RPC, since `WriteToSession` is not a rare RPC; the `ProgramCLIFlagProbeEnabled` precedent is, `feature_flag_service.go:42-45`), **failing closed**: an unreadable or unparsable config keeps the guards ON.
  - *Given* the flag turned off by `UpdateFeatureFlag` (no deploy), *Then* those unary handlers accept a hidden target as on `main` (the stream drops of Story 5.1 are unaffected, and Reply is governed by its own kill switch `hidden_session_reply`, Task 5.6j); *Given* the flag on, *Then* the behavior above holds. **The O7 path stays active and audited in both states, and the audit continues for every hidden steer** (ARCH-C6, ADV C8): while the flag is off a steer to a non-qualifying hidden target (triage, diagnose, `other`, unlinked) is allowed only with an audit line `kind=guard_bypass` (**appended and `fsync`ed before the write, blocking, exactly like Reply**; with the sink down a `guard_bypass` write is **refused** like Reply and is never allowed on a log line alone: review-repair iteration 3, ADV-N18, because the guards-off hatch exists to restore the review composer, not to open writes to triage, diagnose and `other` sessions while the audit is blind), a WARN at most once a minute, `hidden_session_backlog_steer_total{outcome=guard_bypass}`, and the Settings status line ("Hidden-session write guards are OFF: N writes bypassed"); a qualifying review steer takes the typed path either way. The flag is registered in `knownFeatureFlags` as global-only; every `UpdateFeatureFlag` change of it is recorded as a `kind=flag_change` line (previous and new value) by the sink core of Task 2.8h: turning it **back on** (tightening) is persisted first and queued without ever waiting on the sink mutex or an `fsync`; turning it **off** (loosening) is appended and `fsync`ed **before `updateMu` is taken**, under a 2s bound (a stalled `fsync` returns `Internal` with nothing persisted and never holds `updateMu`, so the kill switch cannot queue behind it: ADV-N17), and before it is persisted, **except that it is not refused when the sink itself is down**: it is the escape hatch for exactly that fault, so it is persisted with a `flag_change_audit_degraded` WARN record in the main log carrying the same fields (ADV-N6b, ADR-010 decision 6 degraded modes). With the sink down and the guards **on**, the typed O7 steer and Reply refuse with an explicit message ("Audit log unavailable, steer not sent"; MCP `steer_session` and the terminal remain the fallback); with the sink down and the guards **off**, the qualifying review steer is allowed through the typed path with a `steer_audit_degraded` WARN record in the main log in place of the audit line (`hidden_session_audit_degraded_total{mode=steer_fallback_log}`), and Reply and every non-qualifying (`guard_bypass`) steer still refuse with the same explicit message. State plainly: the guards are a **safety rail, not a security boundary** (a holder of the auth cookie or any process on `:8543` can already create a shell session, steer visible sessions and flip this flag).
- MCP dispatchers are unaffected (characterization). VERIFIED: no `server/mcp` code calls `TerminalService.WriteToSession`; MCP write tools call `session.SendKeysWithTimeout` (`tools_terminal.go:325`) and `inst.SendKeys` (`:402`) directly.
  - *Given* hidden `h1`, *When* the MCP `write_to_session` and `send_control` tools target it, *Then* they behave exactly as before (test pins current behavior); `withDiagnoseGate` is not wired.
- Remaining UI write RPCs are enumerated in ADR-005 (Restart handled in Story 5.1d, `SwitchWorkspace` guarded above; pause/resume/delete unchanged; approval resolution and `RunCommand`-style RPCs listed with their disposition). `ReplyToPendingQuestion` (Story 5.6) and the O7 backlog-steer path of `UpdateSession` are the **only two** UI paths that may write to a hidden session; the guard tests assert that `WriteToSession` and the steer branch for a non-qualifying hidden target still reject in the same test run as a successful reply.
**Files**: `server/services/terminal_service.go`, `server/services/workspace_service.go`, `server/services/session_service_update.go`, `server/services/hidden_review_steer.go`, `hidden_review_steer_test.go`, `server/services/terminal_access.go`, `server/services/feature_flag_service.go`, `server/services/terminal_service_test.go`, `server/mcp/tools_terminal.go`, `server/mcp/diagnose_role_gate.go`, `web-app/src/components/backlog/BacklogItemDetail.tsx` and `web-app/src/components/backlog/detail/SessionsSection.tsx` (read, and the characterization test in `BacklogItemDetail`'s test file).

##### Task 5.2b: Decide access first in `UpdateSession` (before any mutation), guard `terminal_service.go:102` and `SwitchWorkspace`, and split `steerInstance` into the acquirer `steerUnderLease`, the token-gated lease-consuming `steerAuthorized(..., lease, ...)` and the internal acquirer `steerInternal` (lands in one PR with Task 5.2e)
- Use `AccessForUnary(inst, flag GuardsFlag)` (Task 5.2f). In `UpdateSession` evaluate the steer decision right after `findInstanceByID` (`session_service_update.go:156-161`), reject a hidden steer combined with other fields (`steer_must_be_alone`), call the Story 1.8 `LocalWriteGuard` rebinding-gate verdict through the request record for the hidden-steer branch, and obtain the `steerAuthorization` from `decideSteerAccess` (a named function, the only constructor of the `visible` and `backlog_link` kinds); split `steerInstance` (`:371`, which Task 5.0e already made lease-taking) into the acquirer `steerUnderLease(ctx, auth, inst, msg)` (called from `UpdateSession` at the existing `:320` position), the token-gated `steerAuthorized(ctx, auth, lease, inst, msg)` (the former body; **never acquires**; refuses a token whose UUID differs from `inst.UUID`, a zero-value token and a lease for another instance) and the thin `steerInternal`, which `SteerActiveSession` (`session_service_liveness.go:95-101`) calls and which alone constructs the `internal` kind. Lease semantics are Story 5.0's: `steerUnderLease` and `steerInternal` each acquire **once** (`TryTerminalWriteLease`; busy: a retryable `FailedPrecondition`, "a write is in progress, retry") and hand the `*HeldLease` to `steerAuthorized`, whose writing goroutine releases it; neither acquirer calls the other and `steerAuthorized` calls neither (design rule, proved by T-RO-43 and T-WL-02; there is no static rule). Also guard `SwitchWorkspace` (`workspace_service.go:341`) for a hidden target through `AccessForUnary`, **for every switch type and before the `inFlightSwitches` store and `CreateCheckpoint`** (move the in-flight store after the lookup and the guard; inject the `GuardsFlag` into `WorkspaceService` through a setter beside `SetLiveFinder`; the request field is `id`), and in the same access-decision block refuse a change of `program` or `auto_approve` for a hidden target (ADV-N28). The steer branch consults the O7 path (Task 5.2e) before rejecting. Starts only after Task 5.2d is green on `main` and Story 5.0, PR 1f-b (Story 1.8) and PR 2a-2 (Task 2.8h) have merged; **Tasks 5.2b and 5.2e merge in one PR** (between them every backlog steer of a review session fails).

#### Story 5.3: Client read-only mode and reliable deep link
**As an** operator, **I want** "View Session" to open the hidden session read-only every time, **so that** a failure notification is never a dead end.
**Acceptance Criteria** (scope adjusted by Story 1.1):
- Cold deep link with empty live list resolves.
  - *Given* `/?session=<hidden-uuid>&tab=terminal` loaded with `ListSessions` returning `[]`, *When* the page mounts, *Then* `getSession` is called and the session opens (the `sessions.length === 0` early return at `page.tsx:243` no longer blocks the hidden fallback once the list request has settled), with the banner visible within 2000ms of navigation start at V1 and within 4000ms at V2 under 4x CPU throttling on the isolated server (RO-4; initial thresholds, a DECISION to be recalibrated once from the first e2e run).
- Terminal is read-only with banner.
  - *Given* a hidden session open, *Then* the xterm has `disableStdin`, the input bar and soft-keyboard trigger are hidden, and `data-testid="readonly-banner"` reads "Background session - read-only". The banner is announced once through the Announcer (polite, on mount) and renders no live role of its own; its secondary text is "You can read output but not type." when no Reply card is shown (**until PR 5u merges it reads "Terminal input is disabled in this view." instead**, review-repair iteration 6b, ADV-N36: PR 5 ships the stream guards only, while `WriteToSession`, the `UpdateSession` text fields and the O7 steer are guarded by PR 5u, so before then the copy must claim only what this view does; the copy is one constant that PR 5u switches, with its own test, and the primary text "Background session - read-only" is unchanged) and "You can read output. Reply to Claude's question below; nothing else can be typed." when the Reply card is present (RO-12, so the banner never contradicts the card).
- A pending question is answerable from the read-only view (decision O2, Story 5.6).
  - *Given* a hidden session with a pending `INPUT_REQUIRED` question, *Then* the read-only view shows the Reply card (Story 5.6) directly below the banner with the captured question text; the terminal itself stays read-only (`disableStdin`, no input bar). *Given* no pending question (expired, already answered), *Then* no card is shown and the banner stays "Background session - read-only".
- Deleted session degrades, with the notification's own text (C5).
  - *Given* `getSession` returns not found for `/?session=<id>&tab=terminal&notification=<nid>`, *Then* the view shows "Session no longer available" (two buttons >= 44px: Open notifications, Go to sessions) and the notification's captured title and message, looked up by `nid` from the loaded history (hydrated slice first, then `GetNotificationHistory` by id); when `nid` is absent or the record is pruned (cold deep link, a status-change push that has no record, history cleared) it shows only "Session no longer available" and "<title> was removed before you opened it" plus the two buttons, never a 404 and never a blank screen (RO-3).
  - *Given* the toast "View output" action, the tray row "Open" and the Background row, *Then* each builds the link with `notification=<record id>`; *Given* the push payload built by `buildInlineNotification` (`server/push/subscriber.go:193-212`, which has `event.NotificationID`), *Then* `data.url` is `/?session=<id>&tab=terminal&notification=<event.NotificationID>`; the status-change path (`buildStatusChangeNotification`, no record id) and `buildApprovalNotification` keep the two-param URL (`buildSessionURL`, `subscriber.go:302-304`), and the card falls back to the message-less form.
- Hidden items are labelled for the read-only expectation (C6, C13, C15).
  - *Given* a toast, tray row or Background row whose session is hidden, *Then* it shows a text chip "Background" and its primary action reads "View output" (visible text), carrying `data-testid="notification-view-output"` (visible sessions keep "View Session" and `data-testid="notification-view-session"`); e2e and unit tests click by testid, never by visible text. On touch, at most 2 visible actions and no "Focus Window" (Story 3.8).
  - *Given* a hidden triage `INPUT_REQUIRED` notification ("Claude is waiting for your input", screenshot 1), *Then* its toast follows this hidden variant (Background chip, no "Focus Window"); for a hidden `INPUT_REQUIRED` with a pending question the two actions are "Reply" (`data-testid="notification-reply"`) and "View output". (After ADR-003 the idle Notification hook is INFO, so the screenshot's idle toast is suppressed for hidden sessions; the question toast is the AskUserQuestion "Claude has a question" event, `approval_handler.go:771`.)
- Toast, tray row and push use the same link.
  - *Given* a FAILURE notification for hidden `h1`, *When* "View output" is clicked (by testid) from the toast and from the tray, *Then* both land on the read-only view (e2e).
- `IncludeHidden` is never set on the main list.
  - *Given* the main `listSessions` call, *Then* `include_hidden` is false/absent (grep test).

#### Story 5.4: Background activity section in the tray (stacks on PR 4)
**As an** operator, **I want** a glance at what hidden sessions are doing, **so that** I know whether anything in the background is broken without un-hiding them.
**Acceptance Criteria**:
- `hidden_only` list returns only hidden sessions.
  - *Given* 55 hidden and 185 visible sessions in the test DB, *When* `ListSessions{hidden_only:true}` is called, *Then* 55 are returned; `{}` returns 185 (unchanged); `WatchSessions` still drops hidden.
- Section placement and content.
  - *Given* the tray open, *Then* a "Background" segment exists beside "Notifications"; failures and needs-human render as rows with a "Background" text chip and status; routine completions render as one line "12 completed OK today"; its badge counts only failures/needs-human and never adds routine items to the bell count.
- Row join and lifecycle (C7). Background rows are a **filtered view over notification history**, enriched by the session list; they add nothing to the bell count (the same records are already counted there). A hidden session `S` contributes a row when history holds at least one record `R` with `R.session_id` or `R.session_name` equal to `S.id` or `S.title` (`Session.id` is the title today, `proto/session/v1/types.proto:11`; hook IDs are titles, Story 2.2) and `R` is failure-class or needs-human (`ERROR`, `FAILURE` (which includes the Story 2.10 crash notification), `APPROVAL_NEEDED`, `CONFIRMATION_NEEDED`, `INPUT_REQUIRED`, i.e. the types the gate lets through for hidden sessions; there is no STATUS_CHANGE crash record) and `R.is_read == false`. A row **leaves the list** when `R` is read (opening it via "View output" marks it read), dismissed, or cleared (record absent from history). It does **not** leave because the session was deleted or archived: the failure is still true, so the row stays with "Session no longer available" and the captured message, and opens the deleted card (Story 5.3); it leaves once the user reads or dismisses it. "N completed OK today" and "N running" come from `ListSessions{hidden_only}` (status Stopped/Completed with `updated_at` since local midnight and no failure record today; status Running/Ready), and are summary lines, never rows.
  - *Given* hidden `h1` with an unread FAILURE record, *Then* one row appears; *When* the user opens it (record marked read), *Then* the row disappears and the badge drops by 1; *Given* `h1` is deleted while its FAILURE is unread, *Then* the row remains showing "Session no longer available"; *Given* `h2` completed OK with no records, *Then* it contributes only to the "completed OK" count.
- Polling only while visible.
  - *Given* the Background segment collapsed, *Then* 0 `ListSessions` polls; expanded, 1 immediately then every 30s (fake timers).
- Row tap opens the read-only view (Story 5.3). A row whose record is a pending `INPUT_REQUIRED` question also shows a "Reply" action (`data-testid="notification-reply"`, Story 5.6; only once Story 5.6 has shipped, until then every hidden `INPUT_REQUIRED` row shows "View output" only); a row without a pending question does not.
**Files**: `server/services/session_service_crud.go`, `server/services/session_service_crud_hidden_only_test.go` (new file; VERIFIED: no existing `_test.go` references `IncludeHidden`), `web-app/src/components/ui/BackgroundActivity.tsx`, `web-app/src/components/ui/NotificationPanel.tsx`, `web-app/src/lib/hooks/useBackgroundSessions.ts`, `tests/e2e/background-activity.spec.ts`.
