## Repair log (Phase 3, iteration 3: operator decisions and second-round re-reviews; the third plan revision)
Finding ids: C1-C8 are the architecture re-review concerns (`architecture-review.md`), N1-N8 and M1-M6 the adversarial re-review concerns and minors (`adversarial-review.md`), O1-O6 the operator decisions. Every new code claim was checked by opening the file at `013856269` (cited inline); unknowns are Spikes 1.3g-h.

| Finding | What changed |
|---|---|
| C1 index "lock-free" vs `RWMutex` | Decided: copy-on-write. `VisibilityIndex` is an `atomic.Pointer` to an immutable struct; writers take a private writer-only mutex that guards copy-and-swap and calls nothing, so reads are literally lock-free and `Upsert` under `Instance.mu` is safe. Rejected: reword the ADR and keep the `RWMutex`. Story 2.2 ACs (lock-free read, writer under `inst.mu`, copy-cost benchmark), Task 2.2b, Story 2.3 lock-order AC, glossary, Pattern Decisions, ADR-001. |
| C2 refresh back-to-back scans | 5s minimum interval between refresh attempts (success or failure); Story 2.2 AC with a counting fake (at most 4 calls per 20s for 500 misses); `refresh{result}` counter. |
| C3 Gate bound after restore-time producers | The Gate owns its index from construction (no `Bind`); `SeedFromInstances` runs synchronously in `BuildRuntimeDeps` right after `storage.LoadInstances()` (`server/dependencies.go:652`), before the wiring loop (`:876`) and the start loop (`:930`); Task 2.3c rewritten, new Task 2.3d startup test (hidden cold restore yields 0 rows), Story 2.3 AC, "Bus wiring point" revised in Closed items, ADR-001. |
| C4 scan proves presence not use | `TerminalWriter` capability from `access.Writer()`; params structs hold it instead of a `readOnly bool`; scan rules R1 (capability present) and R2 (referenced in the body via `types.Info.Uses`); stated limit; Story 5.1d, Tasks 5.1b/5.1c, ADR-005. |
| C5 scan breadth | Surface derived from the `*session.Instance` method set plus `pane_submit.go` functions with a classification table (an unclassified new method fails the test); adds `SendInputViaControlMode`, `ResizePTYContext`, `SetWindowSizeContext`, `SendPrompt`, `TapEnter` (declarations opened: `session/instance_tmux.go:855,1150,1159,1182,1377,1470,1488,1539,1549`, `session/pane_submit.go:95,141,170`); scope `./server/...` (there is no `server/connectrpc*` directory). |
| C6 `is_pending_decision` for live toasts | The field is also added to the live `NotificationEvent` (`events.proto` field 10, stamped in `server/services/event_converter.go:72`); the fourth client predicate and the client type list are removed; Story 3.2 AC, glossary `PinnedToast`/`is_pending_decision`, ADR-008, `design/ux.md`. |
| C7 `approval.AutoApprovedGate` | Renamed `services.AutoApprovedGate`, declared beside the handler (`approval_handler.go` is `package services`); the no-import criterion applies to `server/push` only. |

Redesigned: one new FAILURE bus producer in `sessionExitedPublisher` (`session_service_events.go:109`) for a Crashed transition; no crash builder on the push path, no `StatusKind`. PermanentlyFailed already has a producer (`session/session_driver.go:1004`, ERROR) and needs a test. Visible crashed sessions also notify (plan assumption, listed in Stage 0, own commit, one-line veto). Story 2.10 re-estimated (cost class, see "Effort"). "Bus path has no producer" claim corrected. |

| NB1 (scan forbade the call the plan requires) | `UpdateSession` is a generated handler method and calls the writer at `session_service_update.go:320`, so "functions on the internal list are not callable from handlers" failed on the plan's own design, and the token meant to separate the UI caller from `SteerActiveSession` was constructible anywhere in package `services`. `steerInstance` is **split** into the token-gated `steerAuthorized(ctx, auth, inst, msg)` (the only steer writer a handler may call; refuses a token whose UUID is not `inst.UUID`) and the thin internal `steerInternal` (caller `SteerActiveSession` only, on the internal list); `decideSteerAccess` is a named function and the only constructor of the `visible` and `backlog_link` token kinds, `steerInternal` the only constructor of `internal`. Scan rules S1 (a handler passes a token that came from `decideSteerAccess`), S2 (construction only in those two functions), S3 (no handler calls `steerInternal` or a list-(2) function), S4 (every listed writer references the write-lease API); automated negative controls (handler constructs a token by literal and by constructor, handler calls `steerInternal`, handler passes a foreign token, listed writer without the lease) plus a positive control (the real `UpdateSession`); the stale "exactly two" wording in the Epic 5 goal, the allowlist bullet, Story 5.6, glossary, plan header, requirements.md, ADR-002, ADR-005 and validation was grepped and fixed (it now says two UI exceptions plus the token-gated and internal classes). C15: the abort flag is a compare-and-swap state word (`idle`, `writing`, `writing-aborted`, `aborted`), stated limit "at most the one write already in the syscall can still land". C16: the write lease is released only when the writing goroutine exits, so an abandoned blocked write cannot overlap the next Reply. C19: order is flag, validate, idempotency lookup (cache, then in-flight), limiters via `Reserve()` with `Delay() == 0`, claim, so a double tap inside 5s returns the first result. C20 and N10: the cache stores the SHA-256 of `(session UUID, question_id, text)`, a mismatch is `InvalidArgument`, the cache and in-flight map are bounded (1024, LRU, 10-minute TTL, INFERRED), `claimed-superseded` state so a release never returns a superseded claim to `confirmed`, and a released claim is re-validated. | NB3 (scan rule S4 against the non-reentrant lease) | The lease is a **passed capability**: `*session.HeldLease`, obtained only from `TryTerminalWriteLease` or `AcquireTerminalWriteLease`; **one acquirer per call chain** (the outermost writer); the write primitives and `steerAuthorized` only receive it; the **writing goroutine owns release**. Rule S4 ("every listed writer references the lease API") is replaced by the typed rules **L1** (a function that calls a classified write takes a `*HeldLease` or acquires), **L2** (the lease is used; the rule checks calls, not references) and **L3** (no function with a lease parameter calls an acquire; no acquirer called from another acquirer or from a lease holder; `HeldLease` literals only in `instance_write_lease.go`), with a runtime backstop `ErrNestedLease` (a panic under `testing.Testing()`). `steerInstance` becomes the acquirer `steerUnderLease`, the lease-consuming token-gated `steerAuthorized(..., lease, ...)` and the acquirer `steerInternal`; `steerHiddenReviewViaBacklogLink` is an acquirer; the later Reply is one. Negative controls: a write with no lease, an unused lease, a double acquire, two acquirers in a chain, a `HeldLease{}` literal elsewhere; positive controls: the three real steer chains, also run on a free and a held lease (T-RO-43). | Finding | What changed (where) |
|---|---|
| NB5 (verified set fed from `d.networks`) | VERIFIED `startRemoteAccess` seeds `networks[ip] = [ip, <raw PTR/avahi names>]` (`main.go:1526`), returned unfiltered (`:1653`), copied into `d.networks` as `InitialNetworks` (`:1676,1702`, `hostname_detector.go:147-151`) and skipped by `resolveAndValidate` (`:287-289`), so `flattenHostnames()` held unverified names and IP literals and the previous T-RP-65 would have passed with the hole open. **Simplest fix, not more machinery**: the verified set is the output of `validateFn` itself, recomputed each detector cycle over the candidates (names in `d.networks` that are not IP literals or `localhost`, plus the boot-time `verifiedHostnames`), and never a copy of any source list; a seeded name is validated, not trusted; IP literals are excluded by `net.ParseIP` because the real `verifyHostnameOwnership` returns true for a local literal. `Server.ReplaceVerifiedHostnames` replaces the add-only setter. Story 1.8 AC (three bullets rewritten, one added), Task 1.8d rewritten, Task 1.8a files (root-package `hostname_detector_test.go`), ADR-010 decision 7, glossary, T-RP-65 reworded, T-RP-70 (seeded unverified name and IP literal, with a `validateFn` that accepts everything), T-RP-71, T-RP-72. |
| m12 (add-only, never re-verified) | The choice is **re-verification every cycle (default 5 minutes), not an expiry**: a name whose record changed drops out at the next cycle and returns when it passes again; a name not yet checked when the cycle times out keeps its previous state; with the detector disabled the set is seeded once at boot and static (stated limit). Story 1.8, Task 1.8d, T-RP-71, T-RP-72, Unresolved Questions. |

## Repair log (Review-repair iteration 5 - simplification)
A **simplification round, not a finding-driven one** (the last repair round; no feature or protection is cut, O8). The previous repair agent concluded that the write-surface guard machinery was partly over-engineered: the type-level capability (`*HeldLease` passed down; `TerminalWriter`, `QuestionReplyWriter`, `BacklogSteerWriter`; the steer token) already makes "forgot to take the lease or the guard" a compile error for every primitive that takes it, which makes a whole-tree L1-L3 scan largely redundant for the lease class, while the five-class partition, the P1-P3 boundary rules and the long pinned lists cost maintenance to guard unreviewed future code, and each review pass found fresh defects in exactly that machinery (NB1, NB3, NB6, ADV-N22, ARCH-C35). Nothing below has had an independent review; nothing is marked verified-resolved.

| Item | What changed (where) |
|---|---|
| Removed from the default design | The five-class partition (`leaseWrite`, `uiWrite`, `primitiveLayer`, `lifecycleExempt`, `noProductionCaller`); rules L1-L3 (lease taken or received, used, never nested); boundary rules P1-P3 (including the `types.Implements` derivation of every `ProcessManager`/`TmuxManager`); steer rules S1-S3 (token from `decideSteerAccess`, construction sites, no handler calls `steerInternal`); the named per-function internal-writer list and the reviewed list (2b) as scan inputs; the whole-tree "unit not in the table fails" derivation; the injectable handler set and its fixture machinery for the scan; the method-set classification tests (T-RO-09, T-RO-29). Story 5.1d, Story 5.0 (the static half of the nesting backstop), Task 5.1d-0/a/c/d, ADR-005 decision 3 and the Effort table were rewritten; the removed design is summarized in ADR-005 "Deferred heavier scan" so the upgrade is cheap. |
| New default (Story 5.1d, ADR-005 decision 3) | Typed capabilities (kept) plus four checks: **(a)** capability-literal lint (no literal, `new`, conversion or initializer-less `var` of `HeldLease`, `steerAuthorization`, `BacklogReviewLink`, `PendingQuestionClaim` or the concrete `TerminalWriter` outside its one defining file; T-RO-36); **(b)** RPC-descriptor classification with `UpdateSession` by field (T-RO-17); **(c)** a short pinned-caller table of the pane-typing primitives, the steer entry points, `AccessForUnary` and the lease acquire API, each row with a one-line reason, failing when a caller is added or removed (T-RO-42, T-RO-45 the closed acquirer list, T-RO-46 lifecycle and wrapper callers); **(d)** `uiWrite` R1/R2 (TerminalWriter present and used in UI stream and handler writers; T-RO-10). Controls: T-RO-12 (negative), T-RO-44 (positive: the real tree passes). |
| Kept (every behavioral protection) | Supersede, fingerprint re-check, per-instance lease (passed `*HeldLease`, one acquirer per chain, goroutine-owned release, `AssertLeaseFree`, no `ErrNestedLease`), steer split (`steerUnderLease`, `steerAuthorized`, `steerInternal`) with its runtime token checks (T-RO-37), the O7 `BacklogReviewLink`, `UpdateSession` `program`/`auto_approve` guard, `SwitchWorkspace` guard, `ForwardScroll` skip, hook proof, host gate, wedge handling, the `terminal_write_lease` flag, and the behavioral test that pins the PR-fix and verdict steers (Task 5.1d-d, T-RO-39). Runtime chain tests T-RO-43, T-WL-02 and T-WL-10 are unchanged and now carry the nesting backstop alone. |
| Task 5.1d-0 | Now a short spike that derives the pinned table from the real tree, records its row count, and **owns the decision whether the heavier scan (or the single rule that closes a gap) is worth adding**; the direction is upgrade only. Unresolved Questions gains the matching open item; Defaults applied gains item 10. |
| Honest blind spots (stated in Story 5.1d, ADR-005 decision 3, Unresolved Questions) | A new pane-typing helper called from a new handler without a capability is caught only by the pinned-caller test and review (and by review alone if it reaches the pane through the PTY layer without calling a primitive-set member); a new `ProcessManager`/`TmuxManager` that writes sits in the by-name exemption; a nested lease acquire in code no chain test runs is not caught statically; "a handler passes a token from `decideSteerAccess`" is no longer a scan rule (runtime UUID check plus pinned callers instead). |
| Inconsistencies found and fixed while editing | (1) ADR-005 said S2 covered "composite literal or `newXSteerAuthorization` call" while the plan said "every expression of the token type"; both now say one lint over all spellings. (2) The plan let `steerInternal` construct the `internal` token while S2 said only two functions may; construction is now confined to one file, `steer_authorization.go`, listed in the Story 5.1d Files. (3) T-RO-32 claimed the scan fails on an `AccessForUnary` call with a bool, which cannot compile against an interface parameter; it now cites the compile error plus the pinned-caller test (T-RO-46). (4) Story 5.0 and Task 5.2b cited "scan rule L3" as the static nesting catch; both now state that nesting is a runtime-tested design rule. (5) ADR-010 decision 9, ADR-010's lease paragraph, ADR-004 decision 14, `requirements.md` scope item 2 and `design/ux.md` Surface 12 still said "scan allowlist", "L1-L3" or "the scan proves"; all aligned. (6) `validation.md` line 4 still said 221 tasks (224 since iteration 4). (7) Plan line 5 and the Verification Gate cited the five-class positive control and S1-S3/L1-L3 controls; reworded. (8) The test file is renamed `terminal_write_guards_test.go` everywhere (it is no longer a scan). |
| PR order and diagram | Re-checked by script (18 nodes, edges as listed in "Order of merges"): acyclic, no edge changed; the 5.1 -> 5.1d-0 -> 5.1d diagram line was re-labelled only. |
| Counts | 6 epics, **42 stories**, **224 tasks** (`grep -c` commands reproduced 42/224/6 on the unedited text; no task added or removed). UX **177** (unchanged). Validation **467** `T-` rows (4 Integration Guard rows deleted: T-RO-09, T-RO-29, T-RO-30, T-RO-38) and **177** UX rows, distinct **639**, split 61 / 255 (of which 20 Guard) / 78 / 38 / 2 / 33, UX tool split 82 / 86 / 8 / 1; the counting script first reproduced the 471 / 177 / 643 block (61 / 255 / 78 / 42 / 2 / 33), 46 requirements covered, 0 unknown ids, matrix mismatch 0; only SC-2, FR-2 and RH-2 matrix rows changed. |
| Estimate (cost classes, no human time) | Story 5.1d goes from M (4xM + 1xS) 575k raw, 479k CU to M (2xM + 3xS) 425k raw, 362k CU (the spike M to S, Task 5.1d-c M to S, Task 5.1d-d M to S). Totals: **15.1M raw (15,140k), 13.1M CU (13,125k) before verification, 19.7-26.3M CU after (midpoint 23.0M), band XL, INFERRED**; -150k raw and -117k CU against the previous 15,290k and 13,242k (story-row sums re-added by script and equal the subtotals). Agent waves: unchanged (about 10-11 for the Epic 5 chain; the spike is shorter but still a serial link). The figure is a ceiling the spike can raise back toward 575k if it adds the heavier scan or one of its rules. |
| Not resolved here | Everything above awaits the re-run of the focused re-review on the patched text (the simplified guard set is unreviewed); Task 5.1d-0 has not run, so the pinned table's real row count and the heavier-scan decision are open; the iteration-4 open items (NB5/NB6 re-review, Spike 1.3g, T-WL-07's PTY unblock, `listen_address`) are unchanged. |

## Repair log (Review-repair iteration 6: pass-5 blockers NB7, NB8 / B1, concerns C42-C50 and N29-N31, minors m14-m19)
Planning artifacts only; no product code touched. Code claims re-opened at `origin/main` `a91e7e46a`. Simplification first: the exec-literal lint is folded into check (a) (so the guard set stays four checks), no `uuid_mismatch` cause is added (the proof's UUID is the attribution, so there is no mismatch), no info/exclude machinery (the proof file is outside the worktree), and the new callback mechanism is replaced by `FeatureController`s. Nothing below has had an independent review.

|---|---|
| NB7 + B1 (capture-pane helpers outside the checks) | Story 5.1d check (d) set now includes `sendInputToTmux`, `sendInputToTmuxWithRetry` and `resizeExternalCapturePaneSession` (exempt only at their definitions); the false sentence "`./server` has no internal caller of these primitives" is replaced by the VERIFIED caller list (`handleCapturePaneInput` `:3350` calls only `sendInputToTmuxWithRetry` at `:3361`; resize `:3375` via `handleCapturePaneResize`; fallbacks `:1414`, `:2150`, `connectrpc_websocket_shell.go:304`); new check (a2), a tmux-subcommand literal lint (`send-keys`, `paste-buffer`, `load-buffer`, `resize-window`, `resize-pane`) outside `session/tmux`, `session/tymux` and the two named definitions; injectable `tmuxInputSender` seam (Task 5.1c-a, params-struct field, no package variable); Story 5.1 AC and Task 5.1a use the seam so T-RO-01's capture-pane row cannot pass vacuously; new row T-RO-48 (hidden managed session, `STAPLER_SQUAD_USE_CONTROL_MODE=false` and `IsManaged == false`: 0 seam calls; named T-RO-44 in the brief, already taken by the positive control); T-RO-12 gains the two negative controls; blind spot (1) reworded; Task 5.1d-0 also lists every other `./server` pane-writing function it finds. Order and PRs: see "PR 5 split" below. |
| NB8 (curl exits 26 on a missing `-H @file`) | VERIFIED here (curl 8.22.0): `-H @/nonexistent` exits 26 with no connection; the guarded command `F=...; set --; [ -n "$STAPLER_SESSION_UUID" ] && [ -f "$F" ] && [ -r "$F" ] && set -- -H "@$F"; curl ... "$@" -d @-` exits 7 (connection refused, i.e. it sent) with the file absent and adds `-H @path` when present, also for a path with a space and a quote. The brief's `$( [ -r F ] && printf -- '-H @%s' F )` form word-splits such a path, so the positional-parameter form is specified instead; same property. C46: no `git add -A` exposure and no `info/exclude` (file outside the worktree); one single-quote escaper for config dir, URL and title (also fixes the existing unescaped title in `buildHookCommand` and `InjectHookConfig`); `InjectHookConfig(rootDir, sessionTitle, sessionUUID)`, `injectHookOptions.sessionUUID`, `RemoveHooksConfig` gains the UUID; the existing local-token `hookAuthArg()` had the same latent fail-closed property and is fixed in iteration 6b (guarded, `upgradeHookAuth` folded into one command producer).
